Another change has been made to the games to make it faster, which is 'tip and run'. 'Tipty' run, 'Tipsy' run or 'Tippy-Go' rule. Isme jab gend balle ko chhuti he to ballebaaz ko bhagna hi hota he chahe yah sparsh jaan buj kar na kiya gaya ho ya bahut hi kam ho. Yah noyam tatkal khel me hi dekha jaa sakta he isme ballebaaz ke dvara gend ko rokne ke adhikaar ko hata kar iski gati ko badhane ki koshis ki gai he.
Kwik cricket me bowler ballebaaz ke taiyar hone ka intazar nahi karta he yah adhik thaka dene vaala khel bachcho ke liye design kiya gaya he. Yah akshar English schools me P>E paath ke liye prayukta kiya jaata he. Is khel ki gati bdhaane ki liye isme ek sanshodhan kiya gaya he ye Tip aur Run “Tipsy” run Tippy-Go Niyam.In this when the ball touches the bat, the batsman has to take a run even if he has not touched the ball deliberately. This rule can only be observed during instant play. In this instead of the batsman slowing the ball by his touch increases the speed.
